## GRAND PRIX UTRECHT 2015

TOP 8 COVERAGE

Davide Vergoni didn't exactly come out of nowhere and certainly was no stranger to the competitive Magic scene before this event. In fact, he started playing Magic twenty years ago, thanks to his brother who taught him the game in 1995. However, this event marked the first time the 30-year-old Italian had been able to reach the Top 8 of a Grand Prix. And he certainly made it count, claimed the title and the trophy, and made Magic history.

The format for Grand Prix Utrecht was, of course, Modern Masters 2015 Edition Limited, one of the most challenging but also rewarding environments for drafters and deck builders alike. Throughout the tournament we had seen powerful artifact decks and green/white token strategies, Spirits and Elementals, but in the end two multicolored decks met in the finals. There, Vergoni's version easily bested Lars Rosengren's version despite the latter's five Tribal Flames

Congratulations to Davide Vergoni, champion (and master) of Grand Prix Utrecht 2015!

DAY 2 COVERAGE

Welcome to Day 2 of Grand Prix Utrecht! (Or Day 4 of the Modern Masters Weekend …) 3,613 players had been in the tournament since Day 1, but only 387 of them managed to escape the Sealed Deck portion with records of 7-2 or better.

These 387 are back for Day 2 for six more rounds split across two *M*odern Masters 2015 Edition** Booster Drafts. After that, the Top 8 players will advance to one more draft and battle it out for the fame, the glory, and their chance to hoist the trophy and claim the title of champion of Grand Prix Utrecht 2015!

DAY 1 COVERAGE

Day 1 of Grand Prix Utrecht 2015, the second leg of our world-spanning Modern Masters Weekend, is in the books. 3,613 players had started the tournament in the morning, but after nine grueling rounds of Sealed Deck play, this number has been reduced to a much more manageable but still impressive 387.

These 387 will return for Day 2 when it's time for *Modern Masters 2015 Edition* Booster Draft. Day 2 involves players battling for the fame, the glory, and their chance to Make Magic History!
